kache: cache hits are restored by COPY because the cache and build tree are on different volumes (C:\ vs F:\), and copy-on-write block-cloning cannot span volumes — so they do not share storage blocks, roughly doubling disk for cached content. Put the cache and build dir on the SAME volume (ideally a ReFS Dev Drive) for zero-copy dedup. If this layout is intentional, silence this advice with `[cache] storage_layout_advice = false` (clone faults are still reported).
